              <!-- ◆ 收藏切换按钮（独立点击不打开详情抽屉） -->
              <span
                role=\"button\"
                tabindex=\"-1\"
                data-test=\"build-card-fav-btn\"
                class=\"ra-card-fav flex-none rounded-[2px] px-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] leading-none transition-opacit
y\"
                :class=\"
                  item.isFavorite
                    ? 'text-[var(--rac-warn)] opacity-100'
                    : 'text-[var(--rac-muted)] opacity-35 group-hover:opacity-90 hover:text-[var(--rac-hi)]'
                \"
                :title=\"item.isFavorite ? '取消收藏（不再置顶）' : '收藏该项目（列表内置顶，支持一键筛选）'\"
                @click.stop=\"onToggleFavorite(item)\"
              >
                {{ item.isFavorite ? '◆' : '◇' }}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- 中部：造价与电力/资源需求 -->
          <div class=\"relative z-10 flex items-center justify-between font-mono text-[11px] leading-none\">
            <span class=\"font-semibold text-[var(--rac-hi)]\">${{ item.cost }}</span>
            <span
              v-if=\"item.power !== undefined\"
              class=\"inline-flex items-center\"
              :class=\"item.power < 0 ? 'text-[var(--rac-ok)]' : item.power > 0 ? 'text-[var(--rac-warn)]' : 'text-[var
(--rac-muted)]'\"
            >
              <Icon name=\"power\" :size=\"11\" class=\"mr-0.5\" />{{ item.power < 0 ? `+${Math.abs(item.power)}` : ite
m.power > 0 ? `-${item.power}` : '0' }}
            </span>
          </div>

          <!-- 底部：在册条目出同源状态谓语（进度X%；尚需T／批次三段式／待收讫），已达上限出不可再生产提示，其余出前置摘要 -->
          <div class=\"relative z-10 flex items-center justify-between truncate text-[11px] leading-tight text-[var(--r
ac-muted)]\">
            <span class=\"truncate\">{{
              item.queueModel
                ? item.queueModel.predicate
                : item.isMaxedOut
                  ? `已达上限 (${item.builtCount}/${item.maxCount}) · 不可再生产`
                  : item.prereq || item.meta || '常规列装'
            }}</span>
            <span
              v-if=\"item.tags && item.tags.length\"
              class=\"ra-card-tag-pill ml-1 flex-none rounded-[1px] border border-[var(--rac-accent)]/20 bg-[var(--rac-
accent)]/10 px-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-[var(--rac-accent)]/80\"
            >
              {{ item.tags[0] }}
            </span>
          </div>
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- ══ 最底侧翻页键（经典双三角 ▲ 与 ▼；flex-none 永远钉底，绝不被网格顶开） ══ -->
      <!-- eslint-disable better-tailwindcss/enforce-consistent-class-order -->
      <footer
        class=\"ra-pager flex-none flex items-center justify-between pt-1.5 px-2 border-t border-[var(--rac-dim)]/40 mt
-1\"
      >
        <!-- 上一页双三角键 -->
        <button
          class=\"ra-page-btn flex items-center gap-1 rounded-[2px] px-2 py-1\"
          :disabled=\"currentPage <= 0\"
          aria-label=\"上一页\"
          title=\"上一页\"
          @click=\"prevPage\"
        >
          <span class=\"text-[11px]\">▲</span>
          <span class=\"font-mono text-[11px]\">PREV</span>
        </button>

        <!-- 页码指示器 -->
        <div class=\"ra-page-indicator font-mono text-[11px] text-[var(--rac-muted)]\">
          <span class=\"font-bold text-[var(--rac-accent)]\">{{ currentPage + 1 }}</span>
          <span class=\"opacity-50\"> / </span>
          <span>{{ totalPages }}</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 下一页双三角键 -->
        <button
          class=\"ra-page-btn flex items-center gap-1 rounded-[2px] px-2 py-1\"
          :disabled=\"currentPage >= totalPages - 1\"
          aria-label=\"下一页\"
          title=\"下一页\"
          @click=\"nextPage\"
        >
          <span class=\"font-mono text-[11px]\">NEXT</span>
          <span class=\"text-[11px]\">▼</span>
        </button>
      </footer>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang=\"ts\">
import { computed, reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue';

  deriveProducibleWithContext,

  playTacticalStagger,
  scheduleNextFrame,
} from '../../红警指挥系统/ui/motion/useGsapMotion';
import {

  buildBaseMotherBuildingCache,
  buildBuiltCountMap,

  buildCompletedTechAndProtocolSet,
  buildDeployFcvOrderText,
  buildDeployMcvOrderText,
  buildDeployOutpostNodeOrderText,
  buildFactionFilterPills,
  buildPrerequisiteContext,

  buildResearchTierMap,
  buildSubTabsForGroup,
  checkBaseHasFactionMotherBuilding,
  checkBuildingAllowedByAuthority,
  checkHasOperationalConyard,
  checkIsZeroBaseStandby,
  computeBuildQuickStatusCounts,
  computeTraitCounts,
  domainsOf,
  evaluateBaseBuildAuthority,
  filterItemsByFaction,
  formatBuildCardTitle,
  inferBuildEntryFaction,
  inferProductionUnlockTierFromMap,
  isBuildItemFavorited,
  isDuplicateCodexEntry,
  isFcvSiteBuildingName,
  isIndividualCharacterCodexEntry,
  isMcvVehicleName,
  loadBuildFavoritesFromStorage,
  matchBuildQuickStatus,
  parseCodexEntryMeta,
  resolveAvailableMcvInFleet,
  resolveBaseConyardFactions,
  resolveCurrentBase,
  resolveEffectiveFilterScope,
  resolveModernizedProductionUnit,
  resolvePlannedDeploySiteName,
  resolveProductionBuiltCount,
  resolveProductionQueueItem,
  resolveUnlockedFactions,
  saveBuildFavoritesToStorage,
  shouldSkipBuildingEntry,
  sortBuildGridItemsWithFavorites,
  toggleBuildFavoriteInSet,
  isCodexEntryBuildableBySystem,
} from './consoleBuildGridHelpers';
import type {

  BuildQuickStatusFilter,
  CatalogMaidenItemLike,

const props = withDefaults(
  defineProps<{
    snapshot: Schema | null;
    activeBaseIndex?: number;
    initialTab?: MainTabId;
    preLaunchLocked?: boolean;
  }>(),
  {
    activeBaseIndex: 0,
    initialTab: '基建',
    preLaunchLocked: false,
  },
);

const catalog = useCatalogStore();

const gridContainerRef = ref<HTMLElement | null>(null);
const { height: containerHeight } = useElementSize(gridContainerRef);

/** 网格几何常量：固定紧凑行高 60px（经典红警侧栏图标尺寸）+ gap-1.5 行间距 */
const GRID_ROW_HEIGHT = 60;
const GRID_ROW_GAP = 6;

/** 五大类页签（建造面板改版：基建/工业/科研/防御/生产；巨构已退役并全权交由工程系统承载） */
const mainTabs = BUILD_PANEL_GROUPS.map(g => ({ id: g.id as MainTabId, label: g.label, icon: g.icon, domains: g.domains
 }));

/** 生产大类子页签由 tagTaxonomy.PRODUCTION_SUBS 单一事实源供给（'人员' / '装备' / '陆军' / '空军' / '海军'） */

const currentTab = ref<MainTabId>(props.initialTab);
const currentSub = ref<SubTabId>('全部');
const currentPage = ref(0);

const dynamicPageSize = computed(() => {
  const h = containerHeight.value;
  if (!h || h <= 0) return 6;
  const rows = Math.max(2, Math.floor((h + GRID_ROW_GAP) / (GRID_ROW_HEIGHT + GRID_ROW_GAP)));
  return rows * 2;
});

const pageSize = dynamicPageSize;
const codex = computed<CodexEntry[]>(() => props.snapshot?.codex ?? []);
const catalogBuildingNameSet = computed<Set<string>>(
  () => new Set((catalog.buildings ?? []).map(b => b.name.trim()).filter(Boolean)),
);
const builtCountMap = computed(() => buildBuiltCountMap(props.snapshot, props.activeBaseIndex));
const buildingLiveStatsMap = computed(() =>
  buildBaseBuildingLiveStatsMap(props.snapshot, props.activeBaseIndex, catalogBuildingNameSet.value),
);
const activeQueueCountMap = computed(() =>
  buildActiveQueueCountMap(props.snapshot, props.activeBaseIndex, catalogBuildingNameSet.value),
);
const queueMap = computed(() => buildQueueMap(props.snapshot));

function deriveBuildableWithContext<T extends { name: string }>(
  items: readonly T[],
  ctx: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof buildCatalogPrerequisiteContext>>,
): T[] {
  const fn =
    (prerequisitesModule as { deriveBuildableWithContext?: (...args: unknown[]) => T[] })
      .deriveBuildableWithContext ?? catalogDeriveBuildableWithContext;
  return (fn as (items: readonly T[], ctx: unknown, queued?: readonly string[]) => T[])(items, ctx, []);
}

const currentBase = computed(() => resolveCurrentBase(props.snapshot, props.activeBaseIndex));
const currentBaseName = computed(() => currentBase.value?.name?.trim() || resolvePlannedDeploySiteName(props.snapshot))
;

/** 从目录与世界书按需加载机娘能力档案（供 FCV 机娘核心权能动态扩权） */
const loadedGirlDossiers = reactive<
  Record<
    string,
    {
      name?: string;
      model?: string;
      specialSkills?: { name: string; tag?: string; desc: string }[];
      specialEquipment?: { name: string; slot?: string; desc: string }[];
    }
  >
>({});
const loadingGirlDossierKeys = new Set<string>();

watch(
  () => [props.snapshot?.forces?.girls, props.snapshot?.girls] as const,
  ([forceGirls, rootGirls]) => {
    const merged = [...(forceGirls ?? []), ...(rootGirls ?? [])];
    for (const g of merged) {
      const name = g?.name?.trim();
      if (!name || loadedGirlDossiers[name] || loadingGirlDossierKeys.has(name)) continue;
      loadingGirlDossierKeys.add(name);
      void loadGirlDossierDetail(name, { model: g.model })
        .then(detail => {
          if (detail) {
            loadedGirlDossiers[name] = {
              name: detail.name,
              model: detail.model,
              specialSkills: detail.specialSkills,
              specialEquipment: detail.specialEquipment,
            };
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {})
        .finally(() => {
          loadingGirlDossierKeys.delete(name);
        });
    }
  },
  { immediate: true, deep: true },
);

const catalogMaidenItems = computed<CatalogMaidenItemLike[]>(() => {
  const rawItems = (catalog as { dynamicCatalog?: { items?: unknown[] } }).dynamicCatalog?.items ?? [];
  return rawItems.filter(
    (item): item is CatalogMaidenItemLike =>
      Boolean(item && typeof item === 'object' && (item as { type?: string }).type === 'maiden'),
  );
});

const currentBaseAuthority = computed(() =>
  evaluateBaseBuildAuthority({
    base: currentBase.value,
    snapshot: props.snapshot,
    catalogMaidenItems: catalogMaidenItems.value,
    dossierMap: loadedGirlDossiers,
  }),
);

const baseHasDeployedFcvSite = computed(() =>
  (currentBase.value?.buildings ?? []).some(
    b => (b.state ?? '运转') === '运转' && isFcvSiteBuildingName(b.name),
  ),
);

const prerequisiteContext = computed(() =>
  buildPrerequisiteContext(
    props.snapshot,
    props.activeBaseIndex,
    catalog.protocols,
    currentBaseAuthority.value,
  ),
);

const hasRuntimeBases = computed(() => {
  const bases = props.snapshot?.bases ?? [];
  return bases.length > 0 && prerequisiteContext.value !== null;
});
const currentBaseConyardFactions = computed(() => {
  const rawPrimary = (props.snapshot?.command as { primary_faction?: string } | undefined)?.primary_faction ?? '';
  const fallbackFaction = /soviet|sov|苏联|苏军|苏维埃/i.test(rawPrimary) ? 'soviet' : 'allied';
  return resolveBaseConyardFactions(currentBase.value, fallbackFaction, currentBaseAuthority.value);
});
const hasOperationalConyard = computed(() => {
  if (isDebugBypassPrerequisitesActive()) return true;
  const filter = selectedFactionFilter.value;
  if (filter && filter !== 'all' && filter !== 'common') {
    return currentBaseConyardFactions.value.has(filter);
  }
  return checkHasOperationalConyard(currentBase.value, currentBaseAuthority.value);
});

export interface ConsoleBuildItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  baseUnitName?: string;
  isModernized?: boolean;
  modernizedRetrofitName?: string;
  category: MainTabId;
  subCategory?: SubTabId;
  faction?: string;
  cost: number;
  power?: number;
  prereq?: string;
  meta?: string;
  desc?: string;
  tags?: string[];
  builtCount: number;
  maxCount?: number;
  isMaxedOut?: boolean;
  queueModel?: QueueDisplayModel;
  liveStats?: BuildingCardLiveStats;
  activeQueueCount?: number;
  unlockTier?: number;
  dynamic?: boolean;
  grantedByGirlSkill?: boolean;
  grantLabel?: string;
  isFavorite?: boolean;
  title?: string;
}

const favoriteKeys = ref<Set<string>>(loadBuildFavoritesFromStorage());

function onToggleFavorite(item: Pick<ConsoleBuildItem, 'id' | 'name' | 'baseUnitName'>) {
  const next = toggleBuildFavoriteInSet(favoriteKeys.value, item);
  favoriteKeys.value = next;
  saveBuildFavoritesToStorage(next);
}

const unlockedFactions = computed<string[]>(() =>
  isDebugUnlockAllFactionsActive()
    ? ['allied', 'soviet', 'yuri']
    : resolveUnlockedFactions(props.snapshot),
);
const selectedFactionFilter = ref<string>('all');

watch(unlockedFactions, list => {
  if (selectedFactionFilter.value !== 'all' && !list.includes(selectedFactionFilter.value)) {
    selectedFactionFilter.value = 'all';
  }
});

const factionFilterPills = computed(() => buildFactionFilterPills(unlockedFactions.value));

function selectFactionFilter(factionId: string) {
  selectedFactionFilter.value = factionId;
  currentPage.value = 0;
}

const availableMcvInFleet = computed<string | null>(() =>
  resolveAvailableMcvInFleet({
    snapshot: props.snapshot,
    selectedFactionFilter: selectedFactionFilter.value,
    unlockedFactions: unlockedFactions.value,
  }),
);

const isZeroBaseStandby = computed<boolean>(
  () => !props.preLaunchLocked && checkIsZeroBaseStandby(props.snapshot, availableMcvInFleet.value),
);

const plannedDeploySiteLabel = computed<string>(() => props.snapshot?.meta?.location?.trim() || '');

const pendingMcvDeployment = computed(() =>
  (props.snapshot?.pending_deployments ?? []).find(d => d.action === '展开'),
);

const mcvDeployStagedHint = ref('');

function handleDeployMcvClick() {
  const mcv = availableMcvInFleet.value;
  if (!mcv) return;
  const { orderText, hintText } = buildDeployMcvOrderText(currentBaseName.value, mcv);
  fillHostSendInput(orderText);
  mcvDeployStagedHint.value = hintText;
}

function handleDeployFcvClick() {
  const fcv = currentBaseAuthority.value.availableFcvVehicle || '前沿基地建设车';
  const { orderText, hintText } = buildDeployFcvOrderText(currentBaseName.value, fcv);
  fillHostSendInput(orderText);
  mcvDeployStagedHint.value = hintText;
}

function handleDeployOutpostNodeClick() {
  const veh = currentBaseAuthority.value.availableOutpostVehicle || '前哨基地展开车';
  const { orderText, hintText } = buildDeployOutpostNodeOrderText(currentBaseName.value, veh);
  fillHostSendInput(orderText);
  mcvDeployStagedHint.value = hintText;
}

watch([() => props.activeBaseIndex, currentBaseName, hasOperationalConyard], () => {
  mcvDeployStagedHint.value = '';
});

const researchTierByName = computed(() => buildResearchTierMap(catalog.researches));

function inferProductionUnlockTier(prereqs?: readonly string[], hasRequirements?: boolean): number {
  return inferProductionUnlockTierFromMap(researchTierByName.value, prereqs, hasRequirements);
}

const laneKeyForQueue = computed(() =>
  makeProdLaneKeyFor(prodLaneIndexFromCatalog(catalog as unknown as EffectiveCatalog)),
);

function displayOf(q: QueueItem): QueueDisplayModel {
  return buildQueueDisplayModel(q, laneKeyForQueue.value);
}

function cardTitle(item: ConsoleBuildItem): string {
  return item.title ?? formatBuildCardTitle(item, item.tags?.join(', ') || '常规');
}

const catalogCanonNames = computed<Set<string>>(() =>
  buildCatalogCanonNames(catalog.buildings, catalog.productions),
);

const catalogCredibilityIndex = computed(() =>
  buildEntityCredibilityIndex({
    buildings: catalog.buildings,
    productions: catalog.productions,
    researches: catalog.researches,
    retrofits: catalog.retrofits,
    projects: catalog.projects,
    protocols: catalog.protocols,
  }),
);

/** 巨构关键词识别列表（「空间站」已退场：D-C6——本体升格为 V2 仓段化工程「轨道空间站」，建造面板不再单列） */
const MEGASTRUCTURE_KEYWORDS = [
  '超时空',
  '天气控制',
  '气象',
  '核弹',
  '铁幕',
  '心灵控制',
  '基因突变',
  '法式要塞巨炮',
  '小型模块化核反应堆',
  '航天发射中心',
  '火箭制造厂',
  '卫星测控站',
  '间谍卫星上行站',
];

/** 全量条目汇编与分类归集 */
const allItems = computed<ConsoleBuildItem[]>(() => {
  const result: ConsoleBuildItem[] = [];
  const prereqCtx = prerequisiteContext.value;
  const debugBypass = isDebugBypassPrerequisitesActive();
  if (props.preLaunchLocked && !debugBypass) return [];
  const hasRunningBuildings = (currentBase.value?.buildings ?? []).some(b => b.state === '运转');
  const runtimeBasesActive = hasRuntimeBases.value || isZeroBaseStandby.value;
  const conyardActive = hasOperationalConyard.value;

  // 1. 建筑目录 (catalog.buildings)：按主领域分桶，巨构关键词优先
  const baseAuth = currentBaseAuthority.value;
  const derivedBuildingNames = new Set(
    debugBypass
      ? catalog.buildings.map(item => item.name)
      : prereqCtx && conyardActive
        ? deriveBuildableWithContext(catalog.buildings, prereqCtx).map(item => item.name)
        : [],
  );
  const unlockedBuildingNames = new Set<string>();
  const buildingAuthCheckMap = new Map<
    string,
    ReturnType<typeof checkBuildingAllowedByAuthority>
  >();
  for (const b of catalog.buildings) {
    const bMeta = b as { id?: string; tier?: number };
    const authCheck = checkBuildingAllowedByAuthority({
      building: {
        id: bMeta.id,
        name: b.name,
        tier: bMeta.tier,
        cost: b.cost,
        power: b.power,
        desc: b.desc,
        tags: b.tags,
      },
      authority: baseAuth,
    });
    buildingAuthCheckMap.set(b.name, authCheck);
    if (debugBypass) {
      unlockedBuildingNames.add(b.name);
      continue;
    }
    if (!conyardActive || !authCheck.allowed) continue;
    if (derivedBuildingNames.has(b.name) || authCheck.grantedByGirlSkill) {
      unlockedBuildingNames.add(b.name);
    }
  }

  // 预计算当前基地活跃阵营已拥有的阵营专属建筑剥离名集合（如基地有盟军建造厂且目录含「盟军发电厂」，则通用槽位模板「发电厂」自动让位，避免出现两张相同 $800/+200 电厂卡）
  const activeFactionSpecificBuildingBaseNames = new Set<string>();
  for (const b of catalog.buildings) {
    const trimmed = b.name.trim();
    const stripped = stripFactionBuildingPrefix(trimmed);
    if (!stripped || stripped === trimmed) continue;
    const bMeta = b as { id?: string; faction?: string; scope?: string };
    const f = inferBuildEntryFaction({
      id: bMeta.id,
      name: trimmed,
      faction: bMeta.faction,
      scope: bMeta.scope,
      tags: b.tags,
      need_building: b.need_building,
      need_buildings: b.need_buildings,
    });
    if (f !== 'common' && currentBaseConyardFactions.value.has(f)) {
      activeFactionSpecificBuildingBaseNames.add(stripped);
    }
  }

  for (const b of catalog.buildings) {
    const bMeta = b as { id?: string; faction?: string; scope?: string; standalone?: boolean };
    const trimmedBldName = b.name.trim();
    const strippedBldName = stripFactionBuildingPrefix(trimmedBldName);
    const canFallbackToStripped =
      strippedBldName !== trimmedBldName && !catalogBuildingNameSet.value.has(strippedBldName);
    const q =
      queueMap.value.get(b.name) ??
      (canFallbackToStripped ? queueMap.value.get(strippedBldName) : undefined);
    const liveStats =
      buildingLiveStatsMap.value.get(b.name) ??
      (canFallbackToStripped ? buildingLiveStatsMap.value.get(strippedBldName) : undefined);
    const builtCount =
      liveStats?.builtCount ??
      builtCountMap.value.get(b.name) ??
      (canFallbackToStripped ? builtCountMap.value.get(strippedBldName) : undefined) ??
      0;
    const queueCountFromMap =
      activeQueueCountMap.value.get(b.name) ??
      (canFallbackToStripped ? activeQueueCountMap.value.get(strippedBldName) : undefined) ??
      0;
    const activeQueueCount = Math.max(queueCountFromMap, liveStats?.underConstructionCount ?? 0);

    // 若当前卡片为通用槽位模板（如「发电厂」「战斗实验室」），且当前基地所属阵营已有专属同名实现（如「盟军发电厂」「盟军战斗实验室」），且本基地未直接建造/排队该通用名，则不重复展示通用模板卡
    if (
      !debugBypass &&
      runtimeBasesActive &&
      strippedBldName === trimmedBldName &&
      activeFactionSpecificBuildingBaseNames.has(trimmedBldName) &&
      !q &&
      builtCount <= 0 &&
      (liveStats?.underConstructionCount ?? 0) <= 0
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (!debugBypass && runtimeBasesActive && !unlockedBuildingNames.has(b.name) && !q && (liveStats?.underConstruction
Count ?? 0) <= 0) {
      continue;
    }

    const tags = inferItemTags({ name: b.name, desc: b.desc, tags: b.tags });
    if (
      shouldSkipBuildingEntry({
        b: { id: bMeta.id, name: b.name, cost: b.cost, standalone: bMeta.standalone },
        tags,
        builtCount,
        q,
      })
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const faction = inferBuildEntryFaction({
      id: bMeta.id,
      name: b.name,
      faction: bMeta.faction,
      scope: bMeta.scope,
      tags,
      need_building: b.need_building,
      need_buildings: b.need_buildings,
    });
    if (
      !debugBypass &&
      runtimeBasesActive &&
      !q &&
      (liveStats?.underConstructionCount ?? 0) <= 0 &&
      faction !== 'common' &&
      !currentBaseConyardFactions.value.has(faction)
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    const { cat, sub } = bucketBuilding(b.name, b.desc, tags);
    const authCheck = buildingAuthCheckMap.get(b.name);
    const prereqText = b.research ? `需 ${b.research}` : b.need_building ? `需 ${b.need_building}` : '';

    const bldItem: ConsoleBuildItem = {
      id: `bld-${b.name}`,
      name: b.name,
      category: cat,
      subCategory: sub,
      faction,
      cost: b.cost,
      power: b.power,
      prereq: prereqText,
      desc: b.desc,
      tags,
      builtCount,
      liveStats,
      ...(activeQueueCount > 0 ? { activeQueueCount } : {}),
      ...(authCheck?.grantedByGirlSkill
        ? { grantedByGirlSkill: true, grantLabel: authCheck.grantLabel }
        : {}),
      queueModel: q ? displayOf(q) : undefined,
      unlockTier: (b as { tier?: number }).tier ?? (b.research ? 1 : 0),
    };
    bldItem.title = formatBuildCardTitle(bldItem, tags.join(', ') || '常规');
    result.push(bldItem);
  }

  // 2. 生产目录 (catalog.productions)
  const unlockedProdNames = new Set(
    debugBypass
      ? catalog.productions.map(item => stripMassProductionSuffix(item.name) || item.name)
      : prereqCtx && hasRunningBuildings
        ? deriveProducibleWithContext(catalog.productions, prereqCtx)
            .filter(item => Boolean(item.need_building) || Boolean(item.need_buildings?.length))
            .map(item => stripMassProductionSuffix(item.name) || item.name)
        : [],
  );

  const completedTechAndProtocolSet = buildCompletedTechAndProtocolSet(
    props.snapshot?.tech?.done ?? [],
    props.snapshot?.protocols,
  );
  const motherBuildingCache = buildBaseMotherBuildingCache(currentBase.value, currentBaseConyardFactions.value);

  for (const p of catalog.productions) {
    const pMeta = p as {
      id?: string;
      faction?: string;
      scope?: string;
      category?: string;
      max_count?: number;
      aliases?: readonly string[];
    };
    if (pMeta.category === 'doctrine') continue;

    const cleanName = stripMassProductionSuffix(p.name) || p.name;
    const modRes = resolveModernizedProductionUnit({
      cleanName,
      rawName: p.name,
      snapshot: props.snapshot,
      catalogRetrofits: catalog.retrofits,
      precomputedCompletedSet: completedTechAndProtocolSet,
    });
    const effectiveDisplayName = modRes.displayName;
    const q = resolveProductionQueueItem(
      queueMap.value,
      cleanName,
      p.name,
      pMeta.aliases,
      modRes.isModernized ? effectiveDisplayName : undefined,
    );
    const count = resolveProductionBuiltCount(
      builtCountMap.value,
      cleanName,
      p.name,
      pMeta.aliases,
      modRes.isModernized ? effectiveDisplayName : undefined,
    );
    const maxCount = typeof pMeta.max_count === 'number' && pMeta.max_count > 0 ? pMeta.max_count : undefined;
    const isMaxedOut = maxCount !== undefined && count >= maxCount;

    if (!debugBypass && runtimeBasesActive && !unlockedProdNames.has(cleanName) && !q && count <= 0) {
      continue;
    }

    const subCat = inferProductionSubTab({
      name: cleanName,
      needBuilding: p.need_building,
      rawCategory: pMeta.category,
    });
    const tagCategory = subCat === '人员' || subCat === '装备' ? '人员' : '载具';
    const tags = inferItemTags({
      name: cleanName,
      desc: p.desc,
      category: tagCategory,
      subCategory: subCat,
      tags: p.tags,
    });
    const faction = inferBuildEntryFaction({
      id: pMeta.id,
      name: cleanName,
      faction: pMeta.faction,
      scope: pMeta.scope,
      tags,
      need_building: p.need_building,
      need_buildings: p.need_buildings,
    });
    const effectiveNeedBlds = p.need_buildings?.length
      ? p.need_buildings
      : p.need_building
        ? [p.need_building]
        : [];
    if (
      !debugBypass &&
      runtimeBasesActive &&
      !q &&
      count <= 0 &&
      faction !== 'common' &&
      !checkBaseHasFactionMotherBuilding(
        currentBase.value,
        effectiveNeedBlds,
        faction,
        currentBaseConyardFactions.value,
        motherBuildingCache,
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    const prereqSummary = modRes.isModernized
      ? `直产现代改 · 原型 ${cleanName}`
      : p.prereqs && p.prereqs.length > 0
        ? `${p.need_building ? `${p.need_building} · ` : ''}${p.prereqs[0]}`
        : p.need_building;
    const unlockTier = inferProductionUnlockTier(p.prereqs, Boolean(p.requirements));

    const prodQueueCount =
      activeQueueCountMap.value.get(effectiveDisplayName) ??
      activeQueueCountMap.value.get(cleanName) ??
      activeQueueCountMap.value.get(p.name) ??
      0;

    const prodItem: ConsoleBuildItem = {
      id: `prod-${cleanName}`,
      name: effectiveDisplayName,
      baseUnitName: cleanName,
      ...(modRes.isModernized
        ? { isModernized: true, modernizedRetrofitName: modRes.retrofitName }
        : {}),
      category: '生产',
      subCategory: subCat,
      faction,
      cost: p.cost,
      prereq: prereqSummary,
      desc: p.desc,
      tags,
      builtCount: count,
      ...(maxCount !== undefined ? { maxCount, isMaxedOut } : {}),
      ...(prodQueueCount > 0 ? { activeQueueCount: prodQueueCount } : {}),
      queueModel: q ? displayOf(q) : undefined,
      unlockTier,
    };
    prodItem.title = formatBuildCardTitle(prodItem, tags.join(', ') || '常规');
    result.push(prodItem);
  }

  // 3. 动态条目 (codex)
  const catalogNames = new Set<string>();
  for (const b of catalog.buildings) catalogNames.add(b.name);
  for (const p of catalog.productions) catalogNames.add(p.name);
  for (const p of catalog.productions) catalogNames.add(stripMassProductionSuffix(p.name));
  const canonNames = catalogCanonNames.value;
  const credibilityIndex = catalogCredibilityIndex.value;
  const knownPersonNames = new Set(
    [
      ...(props.snapshot?.girls ?? []).flatMap(g => [g.name?.trim(), g.model?.trim()]),
      ...(props.snapshot?.forces?.girls ?? []).flatMap(g => [g.name?.trim(), g.model?.trim()]),
      ...(props.snapshot?.command?.adjutants ?? []).map(a => a.name?.trim()),
    ].filter((n): n is string => Boolean(n)),
  );

  for (const c of codex.value) {
    if (catalogNames.has(c.name)) continue;
    if (!isCodexEntryBuildableBySystem(c)) continue;
    const { cost, power, codexTags, desc, codexFaction, cleanCodexName } = parseCodexEntryMeta(c);
    const codexDomain = c.kind === '建筑' ? 'building' : 'production';
    if (
      isDuplicateCodexEntry(c.name, cleanCodexName, catalogNames, canonNames) ||
      isSystemCatalogEntity(credibilityIndex, c.name, codexDomain, 0.80) ||
      isSystemCatalogEntity(credibilityIndex, cleanCodexName, codexDomain, 0.80) ||
      (c.kind === '建筑' &&
        (isSystemCatalogEntity(credibilityIndex, c.name, 'retrofit', 0.80) ||
          isSystemCatalogEntity(credibilityIndex, c.name, 'project', 0.80)))
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    const q = queueMap.value.get(cleanCodexName) ?? queueMap.value.get(c.name);
    const codexLiveStats =
      c.kind === '建筑'
        ? (buildingLiveStatsMap.value.get(cleanCodexName) ??
           buildingLiveStatsMap.value.get(c.name) ??
           buildingLiveStatsMap.value.get(stripFactionBuildingPrefix(cleanCodexName)))
        : undefined;
    const codexQueueCount =
      activeQueueCountMap.value.get(cleanCodexName) ??
      activeQueueCountMap.value.get(c.name) ??
      (c.kind === '建筑'
        ? activeQueueCountMap.value.get(stripFactionBuildingPrefix(cleanCodexName))
        : undefined) ??
      0;
    const count =
      codexLiveStats?.builtCount ??
      builtCountMap.value.get(cleanCodexName) ??
      builtCountMap.value.get(c.name) ??
      builtCountMap.value.get(stripFactionBuildingPrefix(cleanCodexName)) ??
      0;
    if (c.kind === '建筑' && count > 0 && !q) continue;
    if (runtimeBasesActive && !conyardActive && !q && count <= 0) continue;

    if (c.kind === '人员' || c.kind === '装备' || c.kind === '载具') {
      if (isIndividualCharacterCodexEntry(c, knownPersonNames)) {
        continue;
      }
      const subCat =
        c.kind === '人员'
          ? '人员'
          : c.kind === '装备'
            ? '装备'
            : inferProductionSubTab({ name: cleanCodexName, needBuilding: String(c.params?.['前置'] ?? '') });
      const rawCodexPrereq = String(c.params?.['前置'] ?? '').trim();
      const defaultMotherBld =
        subCat === '人员'
          ? '兵营'
          : subCat === '装备'
            ? '个人装备制造所'
            : subCat === '空军'
              ? '空军指挥部'
              : subCat === '海军'
                ? '海军造船厂'
                : '战车工厂';
      const requiredMotherBlds = rawCodexPrereq ? [rawCodexPrereq] : [defaultMotherBld];
      if (
        runtimeBasesActive &&
        !q &&
        count <= 0 &&
        !checkBaseHasFactionMotherBuilding(
          currentBase.value,
          requiredMotherBlds,
          codexFaction || 'common',
          currentBaseConyardFactions.value,
          motherBuildingCache,
        )
      ) {
        continue;
      }
      const tags = inferItemTags({
        name: cleanCodexName,
        desc,
        category: c.kind === '载具' ? '载具' : '人员',
        subCategory: subCat,
        tags: codexTags,
      });
      const codexProdItem: ConsoleBuildItem = {
        id: `codex-${cleanCodexName}`,
        name: cleanCodexName,
        category: '生产',
        subCategory: subCat,
        faction: codexFaction,
        cost: isNaN(cost) ? (c.kind === '装备' ? 100 : c.kind === '人员' ? 200 : 800) : cost,
        prereq: c.params['前置'] || 'EVA动态列装',
        desc,
        tags,
        builtCount: count,
        ...(codexQueueCount > 0 ? { activeQueueCount: codexQueueCount } : {}),
        queueModel: q ? displayOf(q) : undefined,
        unlockTier: 1,
        dynamic: true,
      };
      codexProdItem.title = formatBuildCardTitle(codexProdItem, tags.join(', ') || '常规');
      result.push(codexProdItem);
    } else if (c.kind === '建筑') {
      const tags = inferItemTags({ name: cleanCodexName, desc, tags: codexTags });
      const codexAuthCheck = checkBuildingAllowedByAuthority({
        building: {
          name: cleanCodexName,
          cost: isNaN(cost) ? 1000 : cost,
          power,
          desc,
          tags,
        },
        authority: baseAuth,
      });
      if (
        !debugBypass &&
        runtimeBasesActive &&
        !codexAuthCheck.allowed &&
        !q &&
        count <= 0 &&
        (codexLiveStats?.underConstructionCount ?? 0) <= 0
      ) {
        continue;
      }
      const { cat, sub } = bucketBuilding(cleanCodexName, desc, tags);
      const effectiveCodexQueueCount = Math.max(codexQueueCount, codexLiveStats?.underConstructionCount ?? 0);
      const codexBldItem: ConsoleBuildItem = {
        id: `codex-${cleanCodexName}`,
        name: cleanCodexName,
        category: cat,
        subCategory: sub,
        faction: codexFaction,
        cost: isNaN(cost) ? 1000 : cost,
        power,
        prereq: c.params['前置'] || 'EVA动态增补',
        desc,
        tags,
        builtCount: count,
        liveStats: codexLiveStats,
        ...(effectiveCodexQueueCount > 0 ? { activeQueueCount: effectiveCodexQueueCount } : {}),
        ...(codexAuthCheck.grantedByGirlSkill
          ? { grantedByGirlSkill: true, grantLabel: codexAuthCheck.grantLabel }
          : {}),
        queueModel: q ? displayOf(q) : undefined,
        unlockTier: 1,
        dynamic: true,
      };
      codexBldItem.title = formatBuildCardTitle(codexBldItem, tags.join(', ') || '常规');
      result.push(codexBldItem);
    }
  }

  const filteredByFaction = filterItemsByFaction(result, unlockedFactions.value, selectedFactionFilter.value);
  const favSet = favoriteKeys.value;
  for (const item of filteredByFaction) {
    item.isFavorite = isBuildItemFavorited(item, favSet);
  }
  return sortBuildGridItemsWithFavorites(filteredByFaction, list => sortBuildGridItems(list));
});

const tabCountsMap = computed<Record<MainTabId, number>>(() => {
  const counts: Record<MainTabId, number> = {
    基建: 0,
    工业: 0,
    科研: 0,
    防御: 0,
    生产: 0,
  };
  for (const item of allItems.value) {
    if (item.category in counts) {
      counts[item.category] += 1;
    }
  }
  return counts;
});

function tabCount(tabId: MainTabId): number {
  return tabCountsMap.value[tabId] ?? 0;
}

const searchQuery = ref('');
const selectedTraits = ref<string[]>([]);
const traitMatchMode = ref<'and' | 'or'>('or');
const filterScope = ref<'current' | 'all'>('current');
const statusFilter = ref<BuildQuickStatusFilter>('all');

const effectiveFilterScope = computed<'current' | 'all'>(() =>
  resolveEffectiveFilterScope({
    explicitScope: filterScope.value,
    searchQuery: searchQuery.value,
    statusFilter: statusFilter.value,
  }),
);

const scopedItems = computed(() => {
  if (effectiveFilterScope.value === 'all') return allItems.value;
  return allItems.value.filter(
    item => item.category === currentTab.value && (currentSub.value === '全部' || item.subCategory === currentSub.value),
  );
});

const subTabs = computed<{ id: SubTabId; label: string; icon: string }[]>(() =>
  buildSubTabsForGroup(currentTab.value, allItems.value),
);

const statusCounts = computed(() => computeBuildQuickStatusCounts(allItems.value));
const traitCounts = computed<Record<string, number>>(() => computeTraitCounts(scopedItems.value));

const hasActiveFilters = computed(() =>
  Boolean(
    searchQuery.value.trim() ||
      selectedTraits.value.length ||
      filterScope.value === 'all' ||
      statusFilter.value !== 'all' ||
      selectedFactionFilter.value !== 'all',
  ),
);

const currentItems = computed(() =>
  scopedItems.value.filter(
    item =>
      matchBuildQuickStatus(item, statusFilter.value) &&
      matchItem(item, {
        selectedTraits: selectedTraits.value,
        traitMatchMode: traitMatchMode.value,
        searchQuery: searchQuery.value,
      }),
  ),
);

function resetFilters() {
  searchQuery.value = '';
  selectedTraits.value = [];
  traitMatchMode.value = 'or';
  filterScope.value = 'current';
  statusFilter.value = 'all';
  selectedFactionFilter.value = 'all';
  currentPage.value = 0;
}

const totalPages = computed(() => Math.max(1, Math.ceil(currentItems.value.length / pageSize.value)));

const pagedItems = computed(() => {
  const start = currentPage.value * pageSize.value;
  return currentItems.value.slice(start, start + pageSize.value);
});

function selectTab(tab: MainTabId) {
  currentTab.value = tab;
  currentSub.value = '全部';
  currentPage.value = 0;
}

function selectSub(sub: SubTabId) {
  currentSub.value = sub;
  currentPage.value = 0;
}

function prevPage() {
  if (currentPage.value > 0) currentPage.value--;
}

function nextPage() {
  if (currentPage.value < totalPages.value - 1) currentPage.value++;
}

watch([currentTab, currentSub, currentPage], () => {
  scheduleNextFrame(() =>
    playTacticalStagger(gridContainerRef.value, 'button, .build-cell, .ra-grid-cell', {
      maxItems: 12,
      duration: 0.15,
      stagger: 0.015,
    }),
  );
});

function openDetail(name: string) {
  openLookup(name, codex.value);
}

watch(
  [
    currentTab,
    currentSub,
    pageSize,
    searchQuery,
    selectedTraits,
    traitMatchMode,
    filterScope,
    statusFilter,
    selectedFactionFilter,
  ],
  () => {
    if (currentPage.value >= totalPages.value) {
      currentPage.value = Math.max(0, totalPages.value - 1);
    }
  },
);
</script>

der-right: 2px solid var(--rac-dim); }

[31m+[39m
[31m+ <script setup lang="ts">[39m
[31m+ import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';[39m
[31m+ import Icon from '../../红警指挥系统/ui/Icon.vue';[39m
[31m+ import type { QuarantineItem } from '../../红警指挥系统/parse/parsePipeline';[39m
[31m+ import { in_tavern } from '../../红警指挥系统/composables/store';[39m
[31m+ import { checkEnv, tav, type CheckIssue } from '../../红警指挥系统/parse/systemCheck';[39m
[31m+ // 关键红线：只 import 消费型的解析编排/隔离区/撤销/对账 API 与 addAlias/buildAuthority；[39m
[31m+ // 严禁 import initParseRunner / parseRunner（楼层事件接线薄层），本组件不重装触发器、不自建 eventOn 监听。[39m
[31m+ import {[39m
[31m+   addAlias,[39m
[31m+   buildAuthority,[39m
[31m+   checkParseQuarantine,[39m
[31m+   clearQuarantine,[39m
[31m+   loadIssues,[39m
[31m+   loadQuarantine,[39m
[31m+   loadReports,[39m
[31m+   parseState,[39m
[31m+   retryFloor,[39m
[31m+   runParseFor,[39m
[31m+   submitFix,[39m
[31m+   swipeSlotMissing,[39m
[31m+   undoFloor,[39m
[31m+   type ParseReport,[39m
[31m+ } from '../../红警指挥系统/composables/useParse';[39m
[31m+ // CTRL-UI-AUDIT-01：对账过滤视角视图模型（纯函数独立文件，对齐 dispatchOrderText.ts 先例）[39m
[31m+ import {[39m
[31m+   AUDIT_VIEW_COPY,[39m
[31m+   RECONCILE_VERDICT_COLOR,[39m
[31m+   RECONCILE_VERDICTS,[39m
[31m+   reconcileReportViews,[39m
[31m+   reconcileVerdictCounts,[39m
[31m+   type ReconcileVerdict,[39m
[31m+ } from '../models/auditViewModel';[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const env = checkEnv(in_tavern);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /* ── 解析与修复（状态在 useParse 模块级 reactive，切子页/切 tab 不丢；这里只持 UI 局部态）──[39m
[31m+  * 本组件无任何 eventOn / watch：楼层自动解析触发器由楼层 App 的 initParseRunner() 唯一安装，[39m
[31m+  * 这里仅通过共享的模块级 parseState（reactive 单例）读态，通过手动操作 API 触发解析/隔离处理，[39m
[31m+  * 跨端不重装触发器，避免与楼层重复接线、重复解析。 */[39m
[31m+ const parse = parseState;[39m
[31m+ const parseR20 = ref<CheckIssue[]>([]);[39m
[31m+ const repOpen = ref<number | null>(null);[39m
[31m+ const undoTarget = ref<number | null>(null);[39m
[31m+ const fixKey = ref<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+ const fixText = ref('');[39m
[31m+ const parseMsg = ref('');[39m
[31m+ const parseErr = ref('');[39m
[31m+ // 方案乙：当前最新 AI 楼当前 swipe 页缺 stat_data 快照的检出（null = 无欠账）[39m
[31m+ const swipeMissingId = ref<number | null>(null);[39m
[31m+ // 记为别名写库时的忙态（与解析忙态分开，避免误禁用解析按钮）[39m
[31m+ const aliasBusy = ref(false);[39m
[31m+ // CTRL-UI-AUDIT-01：对账过滤视角（null = 全部流水，既有呈现零变化）[39m
[31m+ const auditFilter = ref<ReconcileVerdict | null>(null);[39m
[31m+ const auditChips = computed(() => {[39m
[31m+   const counts = reconcileVerdictCounts(parse.reports);[39m
[31m+   return [[39m
[31m+     {[39m
[31m+       verdict: null as ReconcileVerdict | null,[39m
[31m+       label: AUDIT_VIEW_COPY.allLabel,[39m
[31m+       count: parse.reports.length,[39m
[31m+       color: 'var(--t-hi)',[39m
[31m+       hint: AUDIT_VIEW_COPY.allHint,[39m
[31m+       active: auditFilter.value === null,[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     ...RECONCILE_VERDICTS.map(v => ({[39m
[31m+       verdict: v as ReconcileVerdict | null,[39m
[31m+       label: v,[39m
[31m+       count: counts[v],[39m
[31m+       color: RECONCILE_VERDICT_COLOR[v],[39m
[31m+       hint: AUDIT_VIEW_COPY.verdictHint[v],[39m
[31m+       active: auditFilter.value === v,[39m
[31m+     })),[39m
[31m+   ];[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+ // 对账视角命中清单（与全部流水同 slice(0, 10) 容量口径）[39m
[31m+ const auditViews = computed(() =>[39m
[31m+   auditFilter.value === null ? [] : reconcileReportViews(parse.reports.slice(0, 10), auditFilter.value),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+ function toggleAudit(v: ReconcileVerdict | null) {[39m
[31m+   auditFilter.value = auditFilter.value === v ? null : v;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const quarantineFloors = computed(() =>[39m
[31m+   Object.entries(parse.quarantine)[39m
[31m+     .map(([mid, items]) => ({ mid: Number(mid), items }))[39m
[31m+     .filter(f => Array.isArray(f.items) && f.items.length) // 防御：存量稀疏数组形态的 null 空洞[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => a.mid - b.mid),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+ const quarantineTotal = computed(() => quarantineFloors.value.reduce((n, f) => n + f.items.length, 0));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function lvColor(level: string) {[39m
[31m+   return level === 'error' ? 'var(--danger)' : level === 'warn' ? 'var(--warn)' : 'var(--t-faint)';[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ const reportLightCountsCache = new WeakMap<[39m
[31m+   ParseReport,[39m
[31m+   { len: number; greens: number; yellows: number }[39m
[31m+ >();[39m
[31m+ function getReportLightCounts(r: ParseReport): { greens: number; yellows: number } {[39m
[31m+   const events = r.events ?? [];[39m
[31m+   const cached = reportLightCountsCache.get(r);[39m
[31m+   if (cached && cached.len === events.length) return cached;[39m
[31m+   let g = 0;[39m
[31m+   let y = 0;[39m
[31m+   for (const e of events) {[39m
[31m+     if (e.light === '\u{1F7E2}') g++;[39m
[31m+     else if (e.light === '\u{1F7E1}') y++;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   const entry = { len: events.length, greens: g, yellows: y };[39m
[31m+   reportLightCountsCache.set(r, entry);[39m
[31m+   return entry;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ function greens(r: ParseReport): number {[39m
[31m+   return getReportLightCounts(r).greens;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ function yellows(r: ParseReport): number {[39m
[31m+   return getReportLightCounts(r).yellows;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ function fmtVal(v: unknown): string {[39m
[31m+   if (v === undefined) return '—';[39m
[31m+   return typeof v === 'string' ? v : JSON.stringify(v);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ function wrapTag(q: QuarantineItem): string {[39m
[31m+   return q.tag && ['战况', '图鉴', '初始状态'].includes(q.tag) ? q.tag : '战况';[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function refreshParseR20() {[39m
[31m+   parseR20.value = await checkParseQuarantine();[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 方案乙护栏检测：最新 AI 楼当前 swipe 页是否缺 stat_data（被动调用，不建事件监听） */[39m
[31m+ async function refreshSwipeMissing() {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const mid = latestAiFloor();[39m
[31m+     swipeMissingId.value = mid !== null && (await swipeSlotMissing(mid)) ? mid : null;[39m
[31m+   } catch {[39m
[31m+     swipeMissingId.value = null;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 方案乙修复入口：对检出楼当前 swipe 页强制重解析 */[39m
[31m+ async function doRepairSwipe() {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   const mid = swipeMissingId.value;[39m
[31m+   if (mid === null) return;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const outcome = await retryFloor(mid);[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value = outcome === 'parsed' ? `◆ #${mid} 当前消息页已重新提取数据` : `修复结果：${outcome}`;[39m
[31m+     await refreshParseR20();[39m
[31m+     await refreshSwipeMissing();[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ type ChatMsgLite = { role?: string; is_hidden?: boolean };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 中控是全局脚本 iframe：Tavern Helper 禁止在此调用 getCurrentMessageId（会抛[39m
[31m+  * 「不要对全局脚本 iframe 调用 getMessageId」）。「本楼」在中控语境下定义为[39m
[31m+  * 最新一个可见 AI 楼：从 getLastMessageId 向前找第一个非 user、未隐藏的楼。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ function latestAiFloor(): number | null {[39m
[31m+   const glmi = tav<() => number>('getLastMessageId');[39m
[31m+   const gcm = tav<(id: number) => ChatMsgLite[]>('getChatMessages');[39m
[31m+   if (!glmi || !gcm) return null;[39m
[31m+   for (let id = glmi(); id >= 0; id--) {[39m
[31m+     const m = gcm(id)[0];[39m
[31m+     if (m && m.role !== 'user' && !m.is_hidden) return id;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return null;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 手动重解析最新 AI 楼（force 跳过幂等） */[39m
[31m+ async function doReparse() {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const mid = latestAiFloor();[39m
[31m+     if (mid === null) {[39m
[31m+       parseErr.value = '未找到可解析的 AI 楼（或非真机环境）';[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const outcome = await runParseFor(mid, { force: true });[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value =[39m
[31m+       outcome === 'parsed'[39m
[31m+         ? `◆ 已重解析 #${mid}`[39m
[31m+         : `#${mid} 未解析（${outcome === 'not-ai' ? '非 AI 楼/已隐藏，不产生快照' : outcome}）`;[39m
[31m+     // 不搬 `ran.value = false`：体检态归「内容体检」组独立组件，中控台各组件间不共享（作废旧体检报告的责任在体检组）。[39m
[31m+     await refreshParseR20();[39m
[31m+     await refreshSwipeMissing();[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 隔离区出路①：重跑本楼（玩家先改消息原文，重跑天然吃到新内容） */[39m
[31m+ async function doRetry(mid: number) {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const outcome = await retryFloor(mid);[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value = outcome === 'parsed' ? `◆ #${mid} 已重跑解析` : `重试结果：${outcome}`;[39m
[31m+     // 不搬 `ran.value = false`（同上取舍）。[39m
[31m+     await refreshParseR20();[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function openFix(mid: number, qi: number, q: QuarantineItem) {[39m
[31m+   const key = `${mid}:${qi}`;[39m
[31m+   if (fixKey.value === key) {[39m
[31m+     fixKey.value = null;[39m
[31m+     return;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   fixKey.value = key;[39m
[31m+   fixText.value = q.raw;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 隔离区出路②：编辑好的 YAML 走同管道重解析入库（不绕过校验） */[39m
[31m+ async function doSubmitFix(mid: number, q: QuarantineItem) {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const res = await submitFix(mid, q, fixText.value);[39m
[31m+     if (!res.ok) {[39m
[31m+       parseErr.value = res.error;[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     fixKey.value = null;[39m
[31m+     const nq = res.result.quarantine.length;[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value = nq ? `已提交，但仍有 ${nq} 项新隔离（见隔离区）` : `◆ #${mid} 修复已入库`;[39m
[31m+     // 不搬 `ran.value = false`（同上取舍）。[39m
[31m+     await refreshParseR20();[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 低置信入库撤销（二段确认后） */[39m
[31m+ async function doUndo(r: ParseReport) {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const ok = await undoFloor(r);[39m
[31m+     undoTarget.value = null;[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value = ok ? `◆ 已撤销 #${r.message_id} 的自动纠错入库` : '撤销不可用（仅真机且未撤销过）';[39m
[31m+     // 不搬 `ran.value = false`（同上取舍）。[39m
[31m+     await refreshParseR20();[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 清理指向已删楼层的隔离残留 */[39m
[31m+ async function doClearQuarantine(mid: number) {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     await clearQuarantine(mid);[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value = `◆ 已清理 #${mid} 隔离残留`;[39m
[31m+     // 不搬 `ran.value = false`（同上取舍）。[39m
[31m+     await refreshParseR20();[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * R19 低置信对齐 → 一键固化进词典（「解析」组两子页联动）。[39m
[31m+  * 写库成功即可：别名子页 PanelAliasDict onMounted 时 loadAliases 会重读到，两子页不强耦合。[39m
[31m+  * buildAuthority 由 addAlias 路径内部无需再显式调用；此处保留 import 供权威名单一致性说明与类型对齐。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ async function doLearnAlias(a: { from: string; to: string }) {[39m
[31m+   parseMsg.value = '';[39m
[31m+   parseErr.value = '';[39m
[31m+   aliasBusy.value = true;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     // buildAuthority 参与权威名单口径对齐（与楼层同源）；记为别名走显式 UI 学习，不强校验拦截。[39m
[31m+     void buildAuthority;[39m
[31m+     const ok = await addAlias(a.from, a.to);[39m
[31m+     if (!ok) {[39m
[31m+       parseErr.value = '仅真机可用';[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     parseMsg.value = `◆ 已记为别名「${a.from}」→「${a.to}」，后续将自动匹配该标准名称（切到「别名词典」子页可见）`;[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     parseErr.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);[39m
[31m+   } finally {[39m
[31m+     aliasBusy.value = false;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ onMounted(() => {[39m
[31m+   // 隔离区跨会话持久（$parse.quarantine），切子页/重建组件时重读同楼层态；读入后刷新 R20 对账[39m
[31m+   void loadQuarantine().then(refreshParseR20);[39m
[31m+   // CTRL-F1-OBS-01：历史告警跨会话持久（$parse.issues），刷新后恢复进报告流水明细[39m
[31m+   void loadIssues();[39m
[31m+   // CTRL-UI-AUDIT-01：报告流水跨会话持久（$parse.reports 瘦身档），刷新后恢复历史报告（与 loadIssues 竞态由占位行升级口径吸收）[39m
[31m+   void loadReports();[39m
[31m+   // 方案乙：开面板即检出一次 swipe 槽位欠账（被动检测，不建事件监听）[39m
[31m+   void refreshSwipeMissing();[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+ </script>[39m

[31m+               <footer class="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-(--rac-dim) bg-(--rac-panel) p-2.
5">[39m
[31m+                 <div class="font-mono text-[12px] text-(--rac-muted)">[39m
[31m+                   更新方式: {{ patch.operation === 'patch' ? '仅修改变动字段' : '整块替换数值' }}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                 <div class="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                   <!-- 废弃驳回 -->[39m
[31m+                   <button[39m
[31m+                     class="ra-patch-btn ra-patch-btn--reject flex items-center gap-1 rounded-[2px] px-2.5 py-1 f
ont-mono text-[12px] font-bold transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                     :disabled="busyId === patch.id"[39m
[31m+                     @click="rejectPatch(patch)"[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <Icon name="close" :size="11" />[39m
[31m+                     <span>废弃驳回</span>[39m
[31m+                   </button>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   <!-- 暂存推迟 -->[39m
[31m+                   <button[39m
[31m+                     class="ra-patch-btn ra-patch-btn--defer rounded-[2px] px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[12px] font
-bold transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                     :disabled="busyId === patch.id"[39m
[31m+                     @click="deferPatch(patch)"[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <span>暂存推迟</span>[39m
[31m+                   </button>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   <!-- 立即生效 -->[39m
[31m+                   <button[39m
[31m+                     class="ra-patch-btn ra-patch-btn--apply flex items-center gap-1 rounded-[2px] px-3 py-1 font
-mono text-[12px] font-bold transition-colors"[39m
[31m+                     :disabled="busyId === patch.id"[39m
[31m+                     @click="applyPatch(patch, 'manual')"[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <Icon name="check" :size="11" />[39m
[31m+                     <span>{{ busyId === patch.id ? '写入中…' : '立即生效' }}</span>[39m
[31m+                   </button>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </footer>[39m
[31m+             </article>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </section>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   </Transition>[39m
[31m+ </template>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ <script setup lang="ts">[39m
[31m+ import { computed, onMounted, ref, watch } from 'vue';[39m
[31m+ import Icon from '../../红警指挥系统/ui/Icon.vue';[39m
[31m+ import type { DataPatch, Schema } from '../../红警指挥系统/core/schema';[39m
[31m+ import { useDataStore } from '../../红警指挥系统/composables/store';[39m
[31m+ import { appendFloorProcessOp } from '../../红警指挥系统/core/timelineLedger';[39m
[31m+ import { useCatalogStore } from '../../红警指挥系统/composables/useCatalog';[39m
[31m+ import { useFloatingStore } from '../../红警指挥系统/composables/useFloatingStore';[39m
[31m+ import {[39m
[31m+   type DiffChange,[39m
[31m+   fileDataPatch,[39m
[31m+   getAutoApplyPreference,[39m
[31m+   setAutoApplyPreference,[39m
[31m+ } from '../../红警指挥系统/worldbook/worldbookDataFiler';[39m
[31m+ import { updateFloorVars } from '../host/floorVarWrite';[39m
[31m+ import { buildPatchDiffList } from '../models/patchDiff';[39m
[31m+ import {[39m
[31m+   clearFailure,[39m
[31m+   type FailRecord,[39m
[31m+   findFailRecord,[39m
[31m+   patchFingerprint,[39m
[31m+   readCurrentChatId,[39m
[31m+   recordFailure,[39m
[31m+   shouldAutoRetry,[39m
[31m+ } from '../models/patchFailFingerprint';[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const props = defineProps<{[39m
[31m+   open?: boolean;[39m
[31m+   snapshot?: Schema | null;[39m
[31m+   embedded?: boolean;[39m
[31m+ }>();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const emit = defineEmits<{[39m
[31m+   (e: 'close'): void;[39m
[31m+ }>();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const store = useDataStore();[39m
[31m+ const catalogStore = useCatalogStore();[39m
[31m+ const floating = useFloatingStore();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const autoApply = ref(getAutoApplyPreference());[39m
[31m+ const busyId = ref<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+ const showRawMap = ref<Record<string, boolean>>({});[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const pendingList = computed<DataPatch[]>(() => {[39m
[31m+   // 优先从 store 响应式读取，兜底 snapshot[39m
[31m+   const fromStore = store.data?.pending_data_patches;[39m
[31m+   if (Array.isArray(fromStore)) return fromStore;[39m
[31m+   if (Array.isArray(props.snapshot?.pending_data_patches)) return props.snapshot!.pending_data_patches;[39m
[31m+   return [];[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function toggleRaw(id: string) {[39m
[31m+   showRawMap.value[id] = !showRawMap.value[id];[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function formatVal(v: unknown): string {[39m
[31m+   if (typeof v === 'object' && v !== null) {[39m
[31m+     return JSON.stringify(v);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return String(v);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const patchDiffMap = computed<Map<DataPatch, DiffChange[]>>(() => {[39m
[31m+   const map = new Map<DataPatch, DiffChange[]>();[39m
[31m+   const cat = catalogStore.effectiveCatalog;[39m
[31m+   for (const patch of pendingList.value) {[39m
[31m+     map.set(patch, buildPatchDiffList(cat, patch));[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return map;[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 为单个 patch 计算可视化的 Diff 变更列表[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ function getDiffList(patch: DataPatch): DiffChange[] {[39m
[31m+   // 尝试从 catalogStore 寻找原建筑或科技（F5：useCatalog 导出名为 effectiveCatalog，科技字段为 researches）[39m
[31m+   const cached = patchDiffMap.value.get(patch);[39m
[31m+   if (cached) return cached;[39m
[31m+   return buildPatchDiffList(catalogStore.effectiveCatalog, patch);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function onToggleAutoApply() {[39m
[31m+   setAutoApplyPreference(autoApply.value);[39m
[31m+   if (autoApply.value) {[39m
[31m+     void processAutoApply();[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 非阻塞提示（CTRL-PATCH-UX-01：禁用阻塞式弹窗，改 toastr；toastr 不可用时仅记控制台）[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ function notify(level: 'warning' | 'error' | 'success', msg: string) {[39m
[31m+   const t = (globalThis as { toastr?: Record<string, (m: string, title?: string) => void> }).toastr;[39m
[31m+   if (t && typeof t[level] === 'function') t[level](msg, '数据补丁');[39m
[31m+   else console.warn(`[数据补丁] ${msg}`);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 失败指纹记录变更计数（让模板里的 failRecordOf 响应式刷新） */[39m
[31m+ const failTick = ref(0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const patchFailRecordMap = computed<Map<DataPatch, FailRecord | null>>(() => {[39m
[31m+   void failTick.value;[39m
[31m+   const map = new Map<DataPatch, FailRecord | null>();[39m
[31m+   const chatId = readCurrentChatId();[39m
[31m+   for (const patch of pendingList.value) {[39m
[31m+     map.set(patch, findFailRecord(chatId, patchFingerprint(patch)));[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return map;[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function failRecordOf(patch: DataPatch): FailRecord | null {[39m
[31m+   if (patchFailRecordMap.value.has(patch)) {[39m
[31m+     return patchFailRecordMap.value.get(patch) ?? null;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   void failTick.value;[39m
[31m+   return findFailRecord(readCurrentChatId(), patchFingerprint(patch));[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 通道 1：【立即生效】[39m
[31m+  * mode='manual'：驾驶员手动点按，不受失败指纹限制，照常重试；[39m
[31m+  * mode='auto'：Auto-Apply 批处理调用，调用前已由 shouldAutoRetry 过滤。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ async function applyPatch([39m
[31m+   patch: DataPatch,[39m
[31m+   mode: 'manual' | 'auto' = 'manual',[39m
[31m+   options?: { skipRefresh?: boolean },[39m
[31m+ ): Promise<boolean> {[39m
[31m+   if (busyId.value !== null || (mode === 'manual' && autoRunning)) return false;[39m
[31m+   busyId.value = patch.id;[39m
[31m+   const chatId = readCurrentChatId();[39m
[31m+   const fp = patchFingerprint(patch);[39m
[31m+   let refreshedNeeded = false;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const res = await fileDataPatch(patch);[39m
[31m+     if (res.success) {[39m
[31m+       // 成功：从 pending 列表中移除，并清除该内容的失败指纹[39m
[31m+       clearFailure(chatId, fp);[39m
[31m+       const list = store.data.pending_data_patches;[39m
[31m+       const idx = list.findIndex(p => p.id === patch.id);[39m
[31m+       if (idx >= 0) list.splice(idx, 1);[39m
[31m+       if (floating.floorId !== null && readCurrentChatId() === chatId) {[39m
[31m+         const floorId = floating.floorId;[39m
[31m+         await updateFloorVars(floating.floorId, vars => {[39m
[31m+           const sd = (vars.stat_data ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;[39m
[31m+           const raw = Array.isArray(sd.pending_data_patches) ? (sd.pending_data_patches as DataPatch[]) : [];[3
9m
[31m+           const appliedRaw = Array.isArray(sd.applied_data_patches) ? [...(sd.applied_data_patches as DataPatch[
])] : [];[39m
[31m+           const appliedIdx = appliedRaw.findIndex(p => p.id === patch.id);[39m
[31m+           const appliedItem = { ...patch, status: 'applied' as const };[39m
[31m+           if (appliedIdx >= 0) appliedRaw[appliedIdx] = appliedItem;[39m
[31m+           else appliedRaw.push(appliedItem);[39m
[31m+           return {[39m
[31m+             ...vars,[39m
[31m+             stat_data: {[39m
[31m+               ...sd,[39m
[31m+               pending_data_patches: raw.filter(p => p.id !== patch.id),[39m
[31m+               applied_data_patches: appliedRaw,[39m
[31m+             },[39m
[31m+           };[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         appendFloorProcessOp(floorId, 0, {[39m
[31m+           kind: 'PATCH_DECISION',[39m
[31m+           ts: Date.now(),[39m
[31m+           patchId: patch.id,[39m
[31m+           patchTarget: patch.target,[39m
[31m+           decision: 'approve',[39m
[31m+           patchSnapshot: patch,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         refreshedNeeded = true;[39m
[31m+         if (!options?.skipRefresh) {[39m
[31m+           await floating.refresh();[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (mode === 'manual') notify('success', `「${patch.target}」已写入《${res.worldbook}》`);[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       // 隔离：按内容记失败指纹，同一内容（即使重解析换了 id）不再自动重试[39m
[31m+       const msg = res.error || '未知原因';[39m
[31m+       recordFailure(chatId, fp, msg);[39m
[31m+       notify('warning', `补丁「${patch.target}」已隔离${mode === 'auto' ? '，不再自动重试' : ''}：${msg}`);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } catch (err) {[39m
[31m+     notify('error', `补丁「${patch.target}」写入异常：${String(err)}`);[39m
[31m+   } finally {[39m
[31m+     failTick.value++;[39m
[31m+     busyId.value = null;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return refreshedNeeded;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 通道 2：【暂存推迟】[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ async function deferPatch(patch: DataPatch) {[39m
[31m+   if (busyId.value !== null || autoRunning) return;[39m
[31m+   const chatId = readCurrentChatId();[39m
[31m+   patch.status = 'deferred';[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     if (floating.floorId !== null && readCurrentChatId() === chatId) {[39m
[31m+       const floorId = floating.floorId;[39m
[31m+       await updateFloorVars(floating.floorId, vars => {[39m
[31m+         const sd = (vars.stat_data ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;[39m
[31m+         const raw = Array.isArray(sd.pending_data_patches) ? (sd.pending_data_patches as DataPatch[]) : [];[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           ...vars,[39m
[31m+           stat_data: {[39m
[31m+             ...sd,[39m
[31m+             pending_data_patches: raw.map(p => (p.id === patch.id ? { ...p, status: 'deferred' as const } : p)),
[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       appendFloorProcessOp(floorId, 0, {[39m
[31m+         kind: 'PATCH_DECISION',[39m
[31m+         ts: Date.now(),[39m
[31m+         patchId: patch.id,[39m
[31m+         patchTarget: patch.target,[39m
[31m+         decision: 'defer',[39m
[31m+         patchSnapshot: patch,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       await floating.refresh();[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } catch (err) {[39m
[31m+     notify('error', `补丁「${patch.target}」推迟落盘失败：${String(err)}`);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 通道 3：【废弃驳回】[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ async function rejectPatch(patch: DataPatch) {[39m
[31m+   if (busyId.value !== null || autoRunning) return;[39m
[31m+   const chatId = readCurrentChatId();[39m
[31m+   const list = store.data.pending_data_patches;[39m
[31m+   const idx = list.findIndex(p => p.id === patch.id);[39m
[31m+   if (idx >= 0) {[39m
[31m+     list.splice(idx, 1);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     if (floating.floorId !== null && readCurrentChatId() === chatId) {[39m
[31m+       const floorId = floating.floorId;[39m
[31m+       await updateFloorVars(floating.floorId, vars => {[39m
[31m+         const sd = (vars.stat_data ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;[39m
[31m+         const raw = Array.isArray(sd.pending_data_patches) ? (sd.pending_data_patches as DataPatch[]) : [];[39m
[31m+         return { ...vars, stat_data: { ...sd, pending_data_patches: raw.filter(p => p.id !== patch.id) } };[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       appendFloorProcessOp(floorId, 0, {[39m
[31m+         kind: 'PATCH_DECISION',[39m
[31m+         ts: Date.now(),[39m
[31m+         patchId: patch.id,[39m
[31m+         patchTarget: patch.target,[39m
[31m+         decision: 'reject',[39m
[31m+         patchSnapshot: patch,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       await floating.refresh();[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } catch (err) {[39m
[31m+     notify('error', `补丁「${patch.target}」驳回落盘失败：${String(err)}`);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 自动热应用批处理[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ let autoRunning = false;[39m
[31m+ async function processAutoApply() {[39m
[31m+   if (!autoApply.value || autoRunning) return;[39m
[31m+   autoRunning = true;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const chatId = readCurrentChatId();[39m
[31m+     const list = [...pendingList.value];[39m
[31m+     let needRefresh = false;[39m
[31m+     for (const patch of list) {[39m
[31m+       // 已推迟或命中失败指纹（同一内容曾被隔离）→ 跳过，不再自动重试（自动通道 applyPatch(patch, 'auto') 批处理合并刷新）[39m
[31m+       if (shouldAutoRetry(chatId, patch)) {[39m
[31m+         const applied = await applyPatch(patch, 'auto', { skipRefresh: true });[39m
[31m+         if (applied) needRefresh = true;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (needRefresh) {[39m
[31m+       await floating.refresh();[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } finally {[39m
[31m+     autoRunning = false;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 监听新补丁到达[39m
[31m+ watch([39m
[31m+   () => pendingList.value.length,[39m
[31m+   n => {[39m
[31m+     if (n > 0 && autoApply.value) {[39m
[31m+       void processAutoApply();[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ onMounted(() => {[39m
[31m+   if (autoApply.value && pendingList.value.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+     void processAutoApply();[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+ </script>[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+   z-index: 10010;[39m
[31m+   background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.72);[39m
[31m+   backdrop-filter: blur(4px);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-modal-card {[39m
[31m+   background: var(--rac-panel);[39m
[31m+   border: 1px solid var(--rac-line);[39m
[31m+   box-shadow:[39m
[31m+     0 8px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8),[39m
[31m+     0 0 0 1px var(--rac-dim);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-patch-quarantine {[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-warn) 12%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-diff-item--added {[39m
[31m+   border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-ok) 45%, transparent);[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-ok) 12%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
[31m+   color: var(--rac-ok);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-diff-item--modified {[39m
[31m+   border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-warn) 45%, transparent);[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-warn) 12%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
[31m+   color: var(--rac-warn);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-diff-item--deleted {[39m
[31m+   border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-alert) 45%, transparent);[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-alert) 12%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
[31m+   color: var(--rac-alert);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-patch-btn:disabled {[39m
[31m+   opacity: 0.45;[39m
[31m+   cursor: not-allowed;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ .ra-patch-btn--reject {[39m
[31m+   border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-alert) 55%, transparent);[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-alert) 14%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
[31m+   color: var(--rac-alert);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ .ra-patch-btn--reject:hover:not(:disabled) {[39m
[31m+   border-color: var(--rac-alert);[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-alert) 26%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
[31m+   color: var(--rac-hi);[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   border: 1px solid var(--rac-dim);[39m

[31m+   color: var(--rac-text);[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ .ra-patch-btn--defer:hover:not(:disabled) {[39m
[31m+   border-color: var(--rac-line);[39m
[31m+   color: var(--rac-hi);[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ .ra-patch-btn--apply {[39m
[31m+   border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-ok) 65%, transparent);[39m
[31m+   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--rac-ok) 18%, var(--rac-bg));[39m
